title: "Zoho Books API reference"
sidebarTitle: "API"
description: "API reference for the Zoho Books plugin in Corsair: every `zohobooks.api.*` operation with its input and output types, taken from the plugin's Zod schemas."
---

<Info>
**New to Corsair?** See [API access](/concepts/api), [authentication](/concepts/auth), and [error handling](/concepts/error-handling).
</Info>

## Bank Accounts

### addBankReconciliationAttachment

`bankAccounts.addBankReconciliationAttachment`

Add a reconciliation attachment. Zoho Books API v3.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.zohobooks.api.bankAccounts.addBankReconciliationAttachment({"pathParams":{"account_id":"999999999999999","reconciliation_id":"999999999999999"},"query":{"organization_id":"999999999999999"}});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `pathParams` | `Record<string, custom>` | No | — |
| `query` | `Record<string, custom>` | No | — |
| `headers` | `Record<string, custom>` | No | — |
| `body` | `custom` | No | — |



**Output:** `custom`


---
